# Mesiri V1 Tracker — DPR for construction

> **The single list of V1 work.** Every session (Claude Code or human) checks this file
> before starting V1 work and updates it when claiming and finishing an item.
>
> - **What V1 is:** one feature, an automatic daily progress report (DPR) for construction
>   companies, on production-grade foundations (AI layer, tenancy, infra, superadmin,
>   dashboard). Surfaces: **web dashboard + WhatsApp assistant only**. Mobile app parked.
> - **Why and what was decided:** the architecture doc
>   ([Mesiri — Target Architecture](https://claude.ai/code/artifact/399eaa7f-c055-4e17-b3aa-a916670dd901)),
>   the [database map](https://claude.ai/code/artifact/80bacce6-0497-4a75-aa1b-08a5cd93c259)
>   and the [UI design](https://claude.ai/artifact/WW1bwDkePpTp8EWK2EswBu).
> - **Decisions:** read [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) first; never re-ask or contradict one.
> - **Process:** [CLAUDE.md](../../CLAUDE.md) still applies to every item: investigate,
>   plan, wait for approval, implement, test, push to `main`, report.

## How to use this file with several sessions at once

1. **Pull first.** `git pull origin main`, then read this file.
2. **Pick an item** whose status is `todo` and whose **Depends on** items are all `done`
   (or say `—`). Prefer `Must` over `Later`.
3. **Claim it before working.** Change its row to `claimed`, put your name/session in
   **Owner**, then commit and push *only this file* straight away:
   `git commit -m "tracker: claim BE-15"`. If the push is rejected, pull: if someone else
   claimed it first, pick another item.
4. **One item per session at a time.** Don't edit other people's rows, except to add a note.
5. **While working:** `in-progress`. If stuck on a decision or another item: `blocked`
   with the reason in **Notes**.
6. **When finished and pushed:** `done`, with the commit hash(es) in **Notes**. Commit
   messages for the work itself start with the item ID, e.g. `feat(dpr): BE-15 add site_days`.
7. **New work found?** Add a row at the end of the right section with the next free ID,
   status `todo`, and say so in your report. Don't renumber existing IDs.

**Status values:** `todo` · `claimed` · `in-progress` · `blocked` · `done` · `dropped`
**Size (rough, focused days):** S ≈ 1 · M ≈ 2–4 · L ≈ 5–10. Estimates, not commitments.

(2) `update_pending_card(card_id, item_index, changes)`: the open card's item keeps its tool arguments; the change is merged (worker lines by trade/name), the old draft is taken back, the item's own save tool runs again, the same card is shown again with Save / Cancel; a refused change puts the old entry back. (3) The day summary ("Materials: ...") now lists a material only while one of its entries is not cancelled (`dpr_request_query.only_kept`). (4) Past day report: the date argument accepts "innale"/"ഇന്നലെ" (also `innaleത്തെ`) as yesterday; an unconfirmed past day with a PDF is sent as before (marked "Not confirmed yet"), one with entries but no PDF gets the day's draft lines marked not confirmed, none says there is none. (5) Reply checks: sentence cut abbreviation-safe ("e.g." was ending a sentence so the number guard cut the reply after it); over the cap the model's words are trimmed at a sentence end; "tap Save" only with a Save card; Manglish done-words ("ezhuthiyittund") before Save are asked again once, then the system's sentence; one script per reply and English-in/Manglish-out (asked again once, then the offending sentences go). Labour query lists each trade once (workforce trade mapping, display only). **Not done / not verified:** the draft for a past day is text, not a PDF (a PDF is only rendered after confirmation); the wrongly removed 22 Oct issue in Test Builders was not restored (needs production data access: ops); live re-run of the 30 messages after deploy; the "Materials: Steel, Cement" cause was not reproduced on production rows (the assembly already drops reversed movements), the fix filters by each entry's review state. |

Slice 2 (Live, `pages/LivePage.tsx`): a draft's chip waits in amber ("⏳ … · Waiting for ✅ Save on WhatsApp · not on the report yet", "N waiting for Save" above the sheet); the tap plays it in ("Saved · Manpower" full size, then onto the report); ❌ Cancel strikes the chip out; saving or cancelling a manpower line is never shown as a correction. A rejected draft still nets to zero (rejected + its reversal). Slice 4 (attendance, site log, issues): `workforce.COUNTED_LINE_SQL` counts a line once its report is saved, and an unsaved later report replaces nothing (Labour page, Today headcount, analytics, WhatsApp labour answers); Site log and Issues lists, analytics work/issue counts and WhatsApp "what work was done" answers leave out unsaved entries, while the agent's own picture of today still sees them (`include_unsaved=True` in `today_context.py`) so it never logs a thing twice. A day whose only entries are unsaved is not "missing work/labour" (`site_day.missing_sections`): the evening message says to keep them under Fix something. | WA-251 | **Reliability 1a ([282]): a bad value never crashes a reply** (live test 7 Nov: closing a saved leak answered "The tool failed", in English) | S | Must | — | done | Claude (session 7 Nov) | Done (hash in git log `WA-251`). Root cause: `list_issues` got `status=open`, the column is an enum (`OPEN`), the API raised, and v3's in-process client passed the exception up as `{"error": "the tool failed"}`, which the model repeated. Class fix in one place each: (1) API (`mesiri/http/db_errors.py`, both apps): Postgres 22P02 and asyncpg's argument check are a 422 naming the meant value ("Did you mean 'OPEN'?") or "not a valid id": every route with a free-text filter or id (20+); (2) assistant: `ApiClient` gets the API's 500 back as a response, and a 5xx read reaches the model as a code-written note (`UNAVAILABLE_NOTE`), never an error text; (3) `control_plane.py failures` lists crashed tools (it showed none on 7 Nov). Also `scripts/friction_report.py`, the understanding + friction baseline (7 days: 41 of 124 material save calls refused, 6-7 messages per saved entry for customers). Tests: `test_db_errors_postgres.py` (500 before, 422 after; bad id; guard), `tests/agent_api/test_api_issue_close_282.py` (crash test fails without the fix). **First push (`8adf9b24`) failed its live re-test** (7 Nov 19:01): `get_db_conn`'s transaction re-raises every database error as MesiriError[POSTGRES_UNAVAILABLE], so the handler (written against a stand-in route) never fired, and the model wrote "The tool failed" again from its own earlier reply. Fix 2: the handler follows the cause chain and re-raises anything that is not a bad value (outages stay 500 and reported); the test now goes through the real route and real `get_db_conn` (500 without, 422 with; severity sibling; outage guard); `reply_guard._API_ERROR` cuts the model's "tool failed" family (site tools kept). **Second push (`eedb3d98`) also failed live** (19:12, still `unavailable`): v3's worker calls its own API app (`agent_api.app.build_api_app`), a third app the handler was not on. Fix 3: installed there; guard test asserts all three API apps have it; integration test calls `build_api_app` on real Postgres (fails without). Third push (`a64e6188`) live: the API answered 422 "Did you mean 'OPEN'?" but the model gave up instead of asking again. Fix 4: on a read's 422 that names the meant value, the code asks again itself, once (`agent.meant_params`; only a value the API named and the query sent). **Live re-test passed** on `6f5841e8` (7 Nov 19:26-19:27, TEST company): `list_issues=200`, open issues listed in Manglish, `resolve_issue` prepared, Save -> the leak is RESOLVED in `site_issues`. 1 live run of the 3 the WA-80 rule asks for. |

Sender: pictures before the words; outside the 24-hour window an announcement waits (`waiting_window`, out of the pending queue) and goes on the person's next message, up to its hold (7 days), else skipped `expired_waiting_for_window`; with `mesiri_product_update` approved, that template goes once and the full message follows their reply. Tests: 4 contract (real Postgres), 5 sender unit; browser-checked (queued 7 with 2 pictures). **Ops:** submit `mesiri_product_update` (Marketing) to Meta, then add it to `WHATSAPP_APPROVED_TEMPLATES`. **Not built:** sending to one person by search; scheduling. Rollback: revert; `alembic downgrade 0509`. |

## SS — Self-serve signup & trials

Anyone can message the Mesiri WhatsApp number (or sign up on the web) and get their own
company on a free trial; their team joins by link with the owner's approval. Decided
13 Oct 2026: **anyone can sign up**; **trial length and limits are superadmin settings, not
fixed** (default and per company); **conversion to paid is manual for now**, online payment
later. Every path must use the same company-creation step as the superadmin wizard.

## CC — Cost control: budget vs actual, every day (GM meeting, 6 Nov 2026)

Plan: [cost-control-plan.md](cost-control-plan.md). The client values a DPR alone at a clerk's overtime; he pays for green/red per work item against the budget. Procurement asks from the same meeting are the PRC rows above, re-prioritised in the plan's §7. Decisions D-CC1..D-CC8 in the plan; each row uses the plan's default until answered. **Order (Ilan, 8 Nov):** cost control before a Finance / Accounts module: core (CC-05, CC-07, CC-11, CC-22), then depth (CC-08, CC-18, CC-19, CC-21), then Tally export (NXT-12), then Accounts (V1.1); see the plan's §15.

Plan: [project-start-plan.md](project-start-plan.md). Asked by Ilan 8 Nov: the middle of a project is covered; the start and the end are not. ## MK — Material knowledge as data (Ilan, 7 Nov 2026; design [ADR-material-knowledge](../architecture/ADR-material-knowledge.md))

Why: 33% of v3 material save calls refused (31 Oct - 7 Nov); TMT and M-sand asked site people for sizes they cannot know and saved impossible ones. Knowledge to review: [material-knowledge-inventory.md](material-knowledge-inventory.md).

| ID | Item | Size | Priority | Depends on | Status | Owner | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MK-01 | Design + knowledge inventory | S | Must | — | done | Claude (session 7 Nov) | ADR proposed; **Alan to review** (shared resolver; C3 changes units-and-sizes rule 4); inventory for a civil engineer to tick. |
| MK-02 | Slice 1: reference tables (unit facts, material kinds, plausible ranges) seeded for TMT + M-sand; the size route refuses the impossible with the range | M | Must | MK-01 | done | Claude (session 7 Nov) | Done (hash in git log `MK-02`). Migration 0515 (additive: `ref_unit_facts`, `ref_material_kinds`, `ref_kind_ranges`, each row with its source); `domains/materials/plausibility.py` applies them; `PUT /materials/{id}/unit-conversions` (dashboard, v3, size-request answers) refuses e.g. "1 load M Sand = 60 cum" ("that is 2119 cft ... usually 50 to 600 cft") and a 12 mm bar of 50 kg ("about 10.67 kg"). Unknown kinds/units are never blocked; a bar without its size only gets the wide 2-100 kg range. Not covered: classic's own size writer (`runtime/material_catalog_query.py`, classic gets no features); the refusal comes at Save, not when the size is prepared. Tests: `test_material_plausibility.py` (7), `test_material_plausibility_postgres.py` (4, real route + seed). Extends units-and-sizes rule 3 (Ilan's go, 7 Nov). |
| MK-02b | The impossible size is refused when it is said, not at Save; API reasons never cut mid-word | S | Must | MK-02 | done | Claude (session 7 Nov) | Done `352057f6`. Live 7 Nov 19:57 (MK-02): "1 load = 60 cum" was put on the card, then refused at Save, cut mid-word, in English, with "send it again". Now `GET /materials/{id}/unit-check?one_unit_equals=` answers `plausible` / `implausible` (reason, usual range) from the same reference (the dashboard can use it too); v3 `size_check` asks it before holding a size and tells the model the one question to ask in their language, never to resend; a size the code already knows (a sized bar) is still never asked. `agent._short`: an API reason is cut at a sentence or word (was 200 chars mid-word). Tests: `test_api_size_plausible_mk02b.py` (5), unit-check integration (1). **Live re-test (22:24) found a sibling**: the same size came as `answer_size_request` (an admin answering a waiting size request), which skipped `size_check`; fixed in the follow-up commit (`size_request` reads the request's material and unit, then the same `size_check`). **Live re-test (22:30)**: the model answered "I could not set the unit conversion right now. Please try again in a minute." with NO tool called, copying the outage reply from the chat history; WA-199's `claims_cannot` missed the past tense. Now it knows "could not / couldn't / kazhinjilla / pattiyilla" (Mesiri as the subject only), so the existing retry sends the model to its tools. **Live re-test passed** (7 Nov 22:43, build 28daccb4): "M sand oru load 60 cum aanu" -> no card, one question in Manglish with the usual range ("would be 2119 cft ... sadharanayaayi 50 muthal 600 cft vare ... Bill-il allel supplier paranjathil oru load ethra?"), nothing held, the company's load size unchanged (2 cum). Left: "would be" stayed English in a Manglish reply. 1 live run of 3. |
| MK-02c | The size question in plain words; a size answered as the bill says ("150 cft") saves in one go | S | Must | MK-02b | done | Claude (session 7 Nov) | Done (hash in git log `MK-02c`). Ilan saw "60 cum would be 2119 cft" as jargon: the model is now told only "far more / far less than one load usually is (50-600 cft)", no conversions in its words. And the answer step: the bill says "150 cft" for M-sand stocked in m³; the model may not convert (a size is a number they said), so the answer could not save. `set_unit_conversion`, `answer_size_request` and `unit-check` now take `size_unit_id`: the size as said, converted by the reference unit facts (422 when no standard measure converts it); the card line shows it as said ("= 150 Cubic Feet (= 4.248 Cubic Meters)"). SOP: pass the number as said with `size_unit_id`; a usual range only when the code gives one. Tests: assistant (10), integration (7). |
| MK-03 | Slice 2: `unit_conversion.py` reads the reference tables instead of constants (same answers) | M | Must | MK-02 | todo | — | No behaviour change; existing tests prove it. |
| MK-04 | Slice 3: TMT sizes as items (`kind`, `size_mm`); the only TMT question is the size | M | Must | MK-03 | todo | — | Needs Ilan (shared component). |
| MK-05 | Slice 4-5: one resolver `POST /materials/resolve`, shadow mode, then switch v3 + dashboard; delete `agent_api/materials.py` branches; save what was said (C3) | L | Must | MK-04 | todo | — | Needs Ilan. |
| MK-06 | Slice 6: initial data from documents (bills, BOQ, POs, stock sheet) | L | Should | MK-05 | todo | — | Needs Ilan. |
| MK-07 | Correct wrong saved rows: New India "1 load M-sand = 60 cum" + its receipt; test company "20 TMT = 1 ton", Steel / TMT / Rod steel split | S | Must | — | blocked | — | Changes saved data: Ilan's OK row by row (rows listed in session 7 Nov). New India: ask Midhlaj the real load size. |

| WA-223 | **[221] Messages kept through an AI pause really are kept; a late answer says which message; "try again" never re-runs history; twin projects tidied here** (Ilan, 3 Nov 22:12-23:48 and 5 Nov 12:15-12:38) | M | Must | WA-143 | done | Claude (session 5 Nov) | **Root cause:** (1) `worker.max_tries` = `MAX_ATTEMPTS` (3) for every failure, so a budget pause said "your message is kept" and dropped it about an hour later; (2) the 5 Nov notes WERE retried (12:36 and 12:38, 20 min after 12:15 and 12:18) but answered with no sign of which message ("Shabdam vyakthamaayilla", "Hello Ilan!"); (3) a message given up on was nowhere in the v3 state, so "try again" made the model re-run an old request from the history (Rana Mart). **Fix:** `ai_outage.BUDGET_MAX_ATTEMPTS` (12, `worker.max_tries`) with `budget_defer_seconds` (20, 40 ... then every 2 h: about 18 h); every other AI failure still stops at 3 (`reply_for_attempt`), and a swallowed ingress failure stops at 3 in `worker.py`. The give-up line says it is kept (`outage.kept`), not "send it again". SOP rule 10: a bare "try again" never prepares anything from the history; with no note, ask which.
